## Box concurrency sweep result (2026-09-23T05:16:20Z)
W3 step 3 ran at 517e09d on the verified corpus (shipped as `s3://…/w3/corpus-517e09d.tar`, 2.53 GB incl. the four pinned sources so that `verify` reports 72/0/0 — a deviation from the ~420 MB approved, accepted; sha256 matched after download). Artifacts committed at `research/plan/evidence/w/box/sweep-20260923T050411Z/` (concurrency.log, bench.jsonl, c*.log, raw, samples.tsv). **Blocking finding for the predeclared rule:** F(c) is never 0 — every level fails the same four 112 s clips (c012, c025, c038, c051; 4/64 per level, 8/128 at c=32; 28 in total) with `VLLMValidationError: video item with 20160|21168|21504 embedding tokens exceeds the pre-allocated encoder cache size 16384` (start-up: "Encoder cache … budget of 16384 tokens, profiled with 1 image item"); the client sees HTTP 200 then an SSE error ~9 s later (bench: stream_error_event, platform-attributed). The longest accepted clip is 72 s (14,773 prompt tokens); the API's profile-v1 cap is 120 s, so **this engine configuration rejects clips the API admits — a deterministic configuration limit, not a load effect.** Read literally, no level qualifies and ENGINE_MAX_NUM_SEQS stays 8 ⚠️; with those deterministic failures set aside W(c)=0 holds for c ≤ 16, T ≥ 0.9×2.376 first at c=16 (T 2.206), floor(X)=80 → the rule would give 16 (recorded for the decision only, not claimed). Throughput: video-s/s 7.4 → 17.1 → 27.4 → 39.8 → 46.5 → 50.1 for c = 1..32; out tok/s 85 → 580; TTFT p50 0.68 → 5.97 s; latency p95 6.3 → 22.5 s; peak_running 13 at c=16 and 24 at c=32 (the API server's video preprocessing likely holds requests before the scheduler — unverified). Script issue for W3: the `vllm:num_requests_waiting` regex also matches `…_waiting_by_reason{…}`, so a nonzero waiting peak may be double-counted (zero unaffected); concurrency.sh's corpus precondition only checks the directory. E1 finding: three NASA-derived clips (c007, c019, c027) produce different bytes on the box's AMD EPYC 7R13 than the Intel-pinned digests with the same pinned static ffmpeg and bitexact flags; Blender's CDN returns 403 to the box's AWS egress. 19 SSM command ids in the operator's report. **Decisions:** the encoder-cache rejection is W4's first measured input (raise the encoder cache budget, or lower the admitted video length, or pre-reject at admission — W4 decides with a re-measurement); until then the pilot must not admit videos the engine rejects — recorded as pending input P-20 for G2's validation (temporary cap at the longest accepted length, 72 s, or an explicit W4 setting); E1B qualifies the corpus README's byte-reproducibility claim per CPU. ## tests/q cross-kill between concurrent full-suite runs (2026-09-23T07:09:57Z)
Diagnosis (coordinator, from `tests/q/vkharness.py` and the lanes' logs): every full-suite run without `INFRX_Q_VALKEY_PORT` uses the shared `infrx-q3-valkey` (55462), and the Q3 sigkill drills (`dr13_shape_the_rebuild_after_a_sigkill…`, `valkey_sigkilled_under_queued_and_running_traffic…`) kill and restart that container — so five concurrent runs (the checkpoint gate's api-test, G2's two api-tests, E3B2's api-test, D4's make check) cross-kill each other's server: the `FFF…F.F.F` pattern at ~41–43 % / ~86 % in G4U's track-first and D4's make check is this, not code. Rule sent to D4/G2/G4U/E3B2: set `INFRX_Q_VALKEY_PORT=<port nothing listens on>` and let the harness start `infrx-q3-valkey-<port>` itself (a pre-started container under another name makes the sigkill drills fail: G4U's 2 reds on its private `infrx-g4u-valkey` are exactly that). Suggested ports: D4 55485, G2 55486, G4U 55487, W4 55488, E3B2 56780. The gate's api-test (started 05:5xZ on the shared server) is expected red in tests/q for the same reason; on that signature tests/q is rerun alone on a private port before judging the checkpoint. Open item "Q Valkey runner env passthrough" stands (the harness override reaches mutant copies via the shared runner only).

## CERTIFY-TREE MERGED 4db74b6 (step 11); USER DECISION: video cap 82 s → 1200 s; LONGCLIP lane dispatched; certify polish round dispatched (2026-09-24T18:58:00Z)
Step 11 merged `codex/certify-tree` 9b467f8 at **4db74b6** (8 files +1306/−181; no conflicts), pushed. **User instruction 18:45Z: "change 82s to 1200s, you have all the permissions for database."** Coordinator reading of the constraint (recorded so the decision is executed, not just typed): the gateway cap is the TUNABLE `MAX_VIDEO_SECONDS` (env on the box; default 120; applied in `media/video.py:204` and the profile's `max_duration_s`); no database row holds it (the per-org entitlement name `max_video_seconds` exists in R24's closed set but is not applied on the media path). Raising the env alone would ADMIT clips the ENGINE refuses: profile v1 caps frames at 240 (200,704 px/frame → 196 tokens per 2-frame patch → 23,520 video tokens at 240 frames) and the pinned engine's encoder cache is 16,384 tokens (W4 phase B: hence 82 s at 2 fps). So 1200 s needs (a) a new serving-version candidate raising the encoder budget (W3 rules: measured on the box before adoption), (b) the byte/timeout caps on the path raised as `apply --set` values (a 1200 s clip ≈ hundreds of MB: `video_b64` cannot carry it — `video_url`/upload only; MAX_MEDIA_BYTES 64 MiB, fetch 20 s, preparation 120 s, TTFT 60 s, the 30 s preparation lease), (c) the product consequence that clips over 120 s sample below the trained 2 fps (1200 s → one frame per 5 s) → pending input P-23 for the user. **LONGCLIP lane** (Opus; worktree codex-longclip, branch `codex/longclip` from 4db74b6; d4/55465/55491, MinIO 55725; no box contact) dispatched 18:55Z with those deliverables + a box measurement protocol the coordinator runs in the maintenance window after the soak (current engine refusal evidence → candidate engine → 82/300/600/1200 s clips, 4 concurrent 1200 s, regression clips → restore). **Certify polish round** (the CERTIFY-TREE agent, new branch `codex/certify-polish` from 4db74b6; N1–N5, N7, N9–N11 of its verifier) dispatched 18:57Z. TOKCOST fix round running (B1). Next: TOKCOST fix → verify → step 12 → gate-3 → main → third install (with the LONGCLIP `--set` values only after the candidate is measured and adopted).
